### [1] [Machine Learning-Driven Content Popularity Prediction and Cache Optimization in D2D Clustered Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26119)
*Maede Rezaei,Ahmadreza Montazerolghaem*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出ML CPCO框架，通过机器学习预测内容和集群级内容需求，结合用户参与意愿优化D2D网络中的缓存放置，实现近97%的缓存利用率和更高的命中 rate。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信技术的发展导致智能设备普及和对高质量内容需求的增长，给蜂窝网络的回传链路带来压力。现有缓存方法未考虑用户异质性，常假设网络中用户偏好均匀分布，而实际上不到20%的用户产生80%的多媒体流量。

Method: 提出机器学习驱动的内容流行度预测与缓存优化（ML CPCO）框架，包括：1）使用机器学习算法预测未来内容请求；2）估算集群级内容流行度；3）考虑用户参与缓存的意愿；4）在D2D集群网络中优化缓存放置决策以最大化效率。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在各种网络条件下表现良好，缓存利用率接近97%，在比较方法中最高；同时提供改进的命中率，执行时间可接受，有效减少回传流量并提升用户体验。

Conclusion: ML CPCO框架通过动态预测内容和集群级内容需求并优化缓存放置，在D2D集群网络中实现了高效的内容分发，显著提升了缓存性能并缓解了回传链路压力。

Abstract: Advancements in wireless communication technology have led to the widespread use of smart devices including computers, mobile phones, tablets, wearable devices, and vehicles which has significantly increased the demand for high quality content. This growing demand puts pressure on the backhaul links in cellular networks, resulting in congestion and content delivery delays. To address this, cache enabled networks and edge caching, such as caching in user devices, have emerged as promising solutions to reduce backhaul traffic. By caching content locally and using device to device (D2D) communication for retrieval, content delivery can be made more efficient. However, limited cache capacity requires intelligent content selection strategies. The popularity of the content is dynamic and varies with user preferences, where less than 20% of the users generate 80% of multimedia traffic. Many existing methods fail to consider this user heterogeneity, often assuming uniform preferences throughout the network. This paper proposes a novel Machine Learning Driven Content Popularity Prediction and Cache Optimization (ML CPCO) framework that dynamically predicts user and cluster level content demand, incorporates user willingness to participate in caching, and optimizes cache placement in D2D enabled clustered networks. The system predicts future content requests using machine learning algorithms and estimates content popularity at the cluster level. Based on these predictions, cache placement decisions are made to maximize efficiency. The simulation results show that the proposed approach performs well under various network conditions, achieving a cache utilization rate of nearly 97% the highest among the methods compared. In addition, it offers an improved hit rate with an acceptable execution time, resulting in reduced backhaul traffic and enhanced user experience.

</details>


### [2] [Dot-Flik: A Scalable Edge AI Architecture for Distributed Insect Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26121)
*Mattia Consani,Denisa-Andreea Constantinescu,Åse Håtveit,Titus Venverloo,Fabio Duarte,Carlo Ratti,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于运动信息的帧过滤算法和分布式分层IoT架构，在边缘端预处理实现60-80%帧削减，支持低成本、实时的昆虫种群监测网络部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球昆虫种群数量下降亟需可扩展的连续监测系统，但现有基于视觉的解决方案受限于高硬件成本、高能耗以及对集中式处理或云连接的依赖。

Method: 提出三项贡献：1) 基于时间差分、Gamma校正运动放大和块级运动密度分析的运动信息帧过滤算法，在边缘端丢弃无关帧而无需深度学习推理；2) 分布式分层IoT架构，通过边缘预处理解耦数据采集与AI分类；3) 在低成本商用硬件上进行真实户外部署验证。

Result: 在微风条件下实现60-80%帧削减；维持30 FPS实时运行，计算余量12.8 ms；节能高达22.6%；每个中央节点支持5-6路并发边缘视频流。

Conclusion: 该研究为城市环境中密集、低成本的生物多样性监测网络建立了实用基础。

Abstract: Global insect population declines necessitate scalable, continuous monitoring systems, yet existing vision-based solutions remain constrained by high hardware costs, energy demands, and reliance on centralized processing or cloud connectivity. This article presents three contributions to address these limitations. First, we propose a motion-informed frame filtering algorithm based on temporal differencing, gamma-corrected motion amplification, and block-based motion density analysis that discards irrelevant frames at the edge while preserving insect activity, without requiring deep learning inference on the sensing device. Second, we introduce a distributed, hierarchical IoT architecture that decouples data acquisition from AI classification through this edge-level preprocessing, projecting fractional scaling of central processing requirements and significantly increasing monitoring coverage compared to monolithic single-stream approaches. Third, we validate the complete system through real-world outdoor deployments on low-cost commodity hardware along four axes: real-time performance, network scalability, hardware cost, and energy efficiency under varying wind conditions. Results demonstrate 60-80% frame reduction under light-wind conditions, sustained real-time 30 FPS operation with 12.8 ms of computational headroom, up to 22.6% energy savings, and support for 5-6 concurrent edge streams per central node. These findings establish a practical foundation for dense, low-cost biodiversity monitoring networks in urban environments.

</details>


### [3] [Privacy-Aware Agent Collaboration for Dynamic VR Slice Management in 6G SD-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26123)
*Khaled M. Naguib,Soumaya Cherkaoui,Mahmoud M. Elmesalawy,Ahmed M. Abd El-Haleem,Ibrahim I. Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向6G网络VR切片管理的移动性驱动隐私感知多智能体强化学习框架，在保护用户隐私的同时实现了资源优化分配，相比传统方法吞吐量提升34%，资源消耗减少28%，隐私泄露降低85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络中的虚拟现实(VR)服务需要超低时延和高吞吐量，这对软件定义无线接入网(SD-RAN)的动态资源管理提出了关键挑战，需要在保证服务质量的同时保护用户隐私。

Method: 提出移动性驱动的隐私感知多智能体强化学习(MARL)框架，协作智能体在保护用户数据隐私的前提下最大化端到端VR链路的资源分配；框架集成了移动性预测和信息瓶颈编码器，以促进高效且安全的智能体协作。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传统方法相比，该方法实现了高达34%的吞吐量提升，资源消耗减少28%，隐私泄露降低85%，能够在未来6G环境中保证可靠的沉浸式VR体验。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了6G网络中VR切片管理的资源优化与隐私保护双重挑战，为未来6G环境下的沉浸式VR服务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Ultra-low latency and high throughput are required for Virtual Reality (VR) services in 6G networks, which presents critical challenges for Software-Defined Radio Access Networks (SD-RANs) dynamic resource management. This work propose a mobility-driven, privacy-aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for VR slice management, in which cooperative agents maximize resource distribution over end-to-end VR links while protecting the privacy of user data. Our approach incorporates mobility prediction and an information bottleneck encoder to facilitate effective and secure agent collaboration. In simulations, comparisons with traditional methods are studied which show up to 34\% throughput improvement, 28\% fewer resources, and 85\% less privacy leakage, guaranteeing dependable immersive VR experiences in future 6G environments.

</details>


### [4] [Enhancing FANET Routing Resilience: A Fuzzy-Driven Bio-Inspired Approach and Its Quantitative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26124)
*Xinwang Yuan,Jinshu Su,Yusheng Xia,Congxi Song*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对FANETs网络提出了一种基于改进人工蜂群算法的簇头选举机制、双层信标簇维护方法以及模糊逻辑自适应Hello间隔控制器，并引入参数敏感性指数评估路由韧性，在提升簇稳定性和降低开销的同时揭示了峰值性能与参数稳定性之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在飞行自组织网络中，无人机的高移动性导致拓扑快速变化和链路不稳定，传统的分簇方法难以在高度动态环境中维持稳定的簇结构；同时，缩短Hello间隔虽然能及时捕获链路变化但会显著增加控制开销，现有方法难以在性能与开销之间取得平衡。

Method: 采用多因子优化改进基于人工蜂群算法的簇头选举；引入双层信标机制进行簇维护；提出基于模糊逻辑的Hello间隔控制器，根据网络动态性自适应调整控制消息频率；设计参数敏感性指数量化评估大规模高动态场景下的路由韧性。

Result: NS-3仿真结果表明，所提协议在整体性能上相比常见基线方案具有显著优势，韧性评估框架分析揭示了峰值性能与参数稳定性之间的权衡关系，有效提升了簇稳定性并降低了重配置开销和控制开销。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了FANETs中簇结构稳定性和控制开销的平衡问题，提出的自适应机制和韧性评估框架为高度动态网络环境下的路由协议设计提供了新的思路和方法论支撑。

Abstract: In flying ad hoc networks, the high mobility of unmanned aerial vehicles leads to rapid topology changes and unstable links. Clustering can simplify topology management, yet maintaining stable cluster structures remains challenging in highly dynamic environments. This paper employs multi-factor optimization to enhance the artificial bee colony-based cluster head election algorithm and introduces a dual-layer beacon mechanism for cluster maintenance, thereby improving cluster stability and reducing reconfiguration overhead. Meanwhile, although shortening the hello interval helps capture link variations in a timely manner, it significantly increases control overhead. To address this issue, a fuzzy logic-based hello interval controller is proposed to adaptively adjust control message frequency according to network dynamics, effectively reducing overhead while preserving transmission performance. Furthermore, to quantitatively evaluate routing resilience in large-scale and highly dynamic scenarios, this paper proposes a parameter sensitivity index to characterize both overall protocol performance and its sensitivity to environmental variations. The proposed protocol is compared with common baseline schemes in NS-3 simulations, and the resilience evaluation framework analysis reveals significant advantages in overall performance while exposing the trade-off between peak performance and parametric stability.

</details>


### [5] [Geometric Fairness-Aware Routing for Federated Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26125)
*Ratun Rahman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Geo-FairFed系统，一种结合双曲图神经网络(HGNNs)和联邦优化的几何公平感知路由方法，通过在负曲率流形上学习拓扑感知表示并引入曲率正则化的公平性目标，在6G边缘网络和IoT拓扑中实现了延迟降低20%、能耗减少17%且公平性提升高达21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的6G和边缘智能网络需要在空间分布式设备间实现有效且均衡的路由算法，但现有联邦路由系统往往优先考虑聚合延迟或吞吐量，而忽视了公平性以及网络拓扑的底层几何结构。

Method: 提出Geo-FairFed系统，将双曲图神经网络(HGNNs)与联邦优化相结合。每个节点在负曲率流形上学习包含层次关系和连接不对称性的拓扑感知表示；全局聚合器使用曲率正则化目标函数，最小化路由损失、几何不一致性以及基于Jain公平指数的不平等惩罚项。

Result: 理论分析证明了在有限曲率条件下的收敛保证，并表明所提出的公平性项能产生帕累托改进的路由性能均衡。在动态6G边缘和IoT拓扑上的广泛仿真表明，与最先进的联邦和几何路由协议相比，Geo-FairFed平均延迟降低20%，能耗减少17%，公平性提升高达21%。

Conclusion: 将拓扑嵌入双曲流形并在联邦更新中纳入公平性，可以显著提升大规模网络路由的效率和公平性。

Abstract: Emerging 6G and edge-intelligent networks require effective and balanced routing algorithms among varied and spatially distributed devices. Existing federated routing systems often prioritize aggregate latency or throughput above fairness and the underlying geometric structure of network topologies. This paper describes Geo-FairFed, a geometric fairness-aware routing system that blends hyperbolic graph neural networks (HGNNs) and federated optimization to provide equal performance across edge nodes. Each node learns topology-aware representations on a negatively curved manifold, which include hierarchical relationships and connection asymmetries. A global aggregator next enforces fairness using a curvature-regularized aim that minimizes routing loss, geometric inconsistency, and an inequality penalty based on Jain's fairness index. A theoretical analysis develops convergence guarantees under limited curvature and shows that the proposed fairness term results in a Pareto-improving equilibrium in routing performance. Extensive simulations on dynamic 6G-edge and IoT topologies reveal that Geo-FairFed minimizes average latency by 20\%, reduces energy consumption by 17\%, and improves fairness by up to 21\% when compared to state-of-the-art federated and geometric routing protocols. The study found that embedding topology in a hyperbolic manifold and including fairness into federated updates can significantly enhance the efficiency and equity of large-scale network routing.

</details>


### [6] [HALO: Hierarchical Auction-assisted Learning for Offloading in SAGIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26293)
*Xuli Cai,Poonam Lohan,Sachin Ravikant Trankatwar,Burak Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对三层空天地一体化网络（SAGIN），提出了一种名为HALO的分层拍卖辅助学习框架，用于延迟感知的任务卸载和资源调度，相比现有DRL方法显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含IoT设备、UAV边缘节点和高空平台站（HAPS）的三层SAGIN中，延迟感知的任务卸载和资源调度问题是一个非凸混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）问题，具有内在的NP困难性，需要设计高效的解决方案以捕获细粒度的系统动态并优化性能。

Method: 引入宏-微时隙模型跟踪累积传输和计算进度，提出HALO框架：结合基于拍卖的任务关联与分层近端策略优化（HPPO）进行资源分配，联合优化任务关联和连续资源控制（包括带宽、发射功率和CPU频率分配）。

Result: 仿真结果表明，HALO在不同流量负载下一致优于代表性DRL基线方法：相比PPO平均提升8.7个百分点任务成功率（11.4%相对增益），相比DDPG和SAC分别有32.4%和89.9%的相对改进，展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HALO能够在变化的流量条件下保持稳定高效的性能，非常适合延迟敏感的SAGIN环境，为解决复杂的任务卸载和资源调度问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate delay-aware task offloading and resource scheduling in a three-tier space-air-ground integrated network (SAGIN) consisting of IoT devices, UAV edge nodes, and a high-altitude platform station (HAPS). We formulate joint task association and continuous resource control (including bandwidth, transmit power, and CPU frequency allocation) as a non-convex mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, which is inherently NP-hard. To capture fine-grained system dynamics, we introduce a macro-micro slot model that tracks cumulative transmission and computation progress over time. Based on this model, we propose HALO, a hierarchical auction-assisted learning framework that combines auction-based task association with hierarchical Proximal Policy Optimization (HPPO) for resource allocation. Simulation results under different traffic loads show that HALO consistently outperforms representative deep reinforcement learning (DRL) baselines. In particular, HALO achieves an average improvement of 8.7 percentage points in task success rate over PPO (corresponding to an 11.4% relative gain) and shows consistently greater robustness than DDPG and SAC, with relative improvements of 32.4% and 89.9%, respectively. These results highlight HALO's ability to maintain stable and efficient performance under varying traffic conditions, making it well-suited for delay-sensitive SAGIN environments.

</details>


### [7] [5G NR-V2X Scheduling Approaches for CPM Variable Size Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26746)
*Vittorio Todisco,Mattia Andreani,Maria Luisa Merani,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究基于真实感知数据重构的变长集体感知消息(CPM)流量，评估了NR-V2X Mode 2调度策略。结果表明：资源分配的稳定性比瞬时负载优化更关键；带填充的半持续调度(SPS)性能稳健，而动态调度和激进重选会因频繁资源重分配增加碰撞概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体感知消息(CPM)由于动态对象包含和周期性安全开销引入了显著的包大小可变性。然而，5G NR-V2X Mode 2通常采用针对周期性流量和相对稳定包大小设计的半持续调度(SPS)，现实CPM驱动的尺寸波动对分布式资源分配的影响尚未被充分理解。

Method: 基于真实世界感知数据集重构变长CPM流量，对NR-V2X Mode 2调度策略进行系统级比较评估。分析了动态调度和多种基于SPS的方法，包括基于填充的分配、激进尺寸驱动重选以及调制编码方案(MCS)自适应。

Result: 包大小可变性在调度稳定性受损时会显著降低可靠性。动态调度和激进重选因频繁资源重分配而增加碰撞概率。相比之下，带填充的SPS收敛到稳定的资源分配并提供稳健性能，而MCS自适应实现了最高平均包接收率，但在不同包类型间可靠性不均匀。

Conclusion: 在现实CPM流量下，资源使用的稳定性比瞬时负载优化更为关键。研究为NR-V2X Mode 2上的CPS部署提供了设计指南。

Abstract: Collective perception messages (CPMs) introduce significant packet size variability due to dynamic object inclusion and periodic security overhead. While 5G NR-V2X Mode 2 typically employs semi-persistent scheduling (SPS) designed for periodic traffic with relatively stable packet sizes, the impact of realistic CPM-driven size fluctuations on distributed resource allocation remains insufficiently understood. This paper presents a comparative system-level evaluation of NR-V2X Mode 2 scheduling strategies under variable-size CPM traffic reconstructed from real-world perception datasets. We analyze dynamic scheduling and multiple SPS-based approaches, including padding-based allocation, aggressive size-driven reselection, and modulation and coding scheme (MCS) adaptation. Results show that packet size variability can significantly degrade reliability when scheduling stability is compromised. In particular, dynamic scheduling and aggressive reselection increase collision probability due to frequent resource reallocations. In contrast, SPS with padding converges to a stable resource allocation and provides robust performance, while MCS adaptation achieves the highest average packet reception ratio but with uneven reliability across packet types. The findings demonstrate that, under realistic CPM traffic, stability of resource usage is more critical than instantaneous load optimization, and provide design guidelines for CPS deployment over NR-V2X Mode 2.

</details>


### [8] [An Experimental Assessment of the Spatial and Frequency Selectivity of Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26808)
*Cyrille Morin,Leonardo S Cardoso,Maxime Guillaud,Ahmad Shokair,Youssef Nasser,Amelie Hennequart,Geoffroy Lerosey*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了RIS对其优化链路以外的无线链路的影响，发现即使在不同频率或标称频率范围外，RIS对次级链路的接收功率和信道相位仍有显著影响，载波频率分隔无法缓解该干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注RIS对目标链路的性能优化，但忽视了RIS配置可能对邻近其他通信链路产生的非预期影响。作者旨在填补这一空白，研究RIS在优化主链路配置时对次级链路的潜在干扰问题。

Method: 在FR1频段使用CorteXlab无线测试平台和Greenerwave RIS进行实验验证。考虑三种场景：次级链路与RIS优化主链路处于相同环境、相同频率或不同频率，通过测量次级链路的接收功率和信道相位变化来评估影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，即使次级链路处于RIS标称频率范围之外，RIS配置仍对其产生显著影响，包括接收功率变化和信道相位改变。两种通信链路之间的载波频率分隔无法有效缓解这种干扰。

Conclusion: RIS对非优化目标链路的干扰是一个不可忽视的问题，在实际部署中需要考虑RIS对相邻链路的潜在影响，这对多用户或多系统共存场景下的RIS部署和配置具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: This work investigates the impact of reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) on radio links other than the one for which the RIS configuration is optimized. We consider three different scenarios in which a secondary communication link could be affected by a RIS whose configuration is optimized for a primary communication link operating in the vicinity, on the same or on different frequencies. This question is investigated experimentally in the FR1 band, using the CorteXlab radio testbed and a Greenerwave RIS. We show that the impact, in terms of received power and impact on the channel phase of the secondary link, is significant even outside of the nominal frequency range of the RIS, and is not mitigated by carrier frequency separation between the two communication links.

</details>


### [9] [Toward Agentic SysAdmin: Rethinking System Administration with AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26960)
*Gianmaria Frigo,Davide Saladino,Alberto Castagnaro,Francesco Marchiori,Denis Donadel,Luca Pajola,Mauro Conti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NetLLMeval框架，通过实时网络模拟自动评估大语言模型在网络管理任务中的表现，发现求解器设计显著影响准确性，且本地部署的中小型模型在合适配置下可媲美万亿参数级前沿系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着云原生架构、异构设备和分布式系统的普及，网络管理复杂度日益增加，管理员面临配置、故障排查和安全的巨大压力。LLM虽有望辅助或部分自动化这些任务，但现有基准测试依赖静态参考输出或人工验证，无法适应真实网络状态的多样性和LLM部署策略（从单次提示到智能体流水线）的差异性，因此亟需可扩展的系统评估方法。

Method: 提出NetLLMeval框架，利用实时网络模拟自动获取真实值（ground truth），无需人工干预即可对LLM系统进行评测。研究采用全因子实验设计，涵盖10个基础模型、4种求解器架构、10种任务类型和6种复杂度递增的网络拓扑，共进行24000次实验运行。

Result: 实验结果表明，求解器设计对准确性影响巨大：一个14B参数的开源模型正确率从0.43提升至0.88；在合适配置下，本地可部署的中小型模型可达到万亿参数级前沿系统的性能水平。

Conclusion: NetLLMeval为LLM在网络管理任务中的评估提供了可扩展、可复现的自动化基准，证明求解器设计是提升性能的关键因素，中小型模型在优化配置下具备实用价值。框架已开源以支持未来模型和求解器设计的可复现评估。

Abstract: The growing complexity of computer networks, driven by cloud-native architectures, heterogeneous devices, and distributed systems, places increasing pressure on network administrators who must simultaneously manage configuration, troubleshooting, and security under tight operational constraints. Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as a promising tool to assist and partially automate these tasks, yet their systematic evaluation in networking scenarios remains an open challenge. Existing benchmarks rely on static reference outputs or manual expert validation, neither of which scales to the diversity of real-world network states or to the variety of orchestration strategies -- from monolithic prompting to fully agentic pipelines --through which LLMs are increasingly deployed.
  In this paper, we present NetLLMeval, a framework for automatically evaluating LLM-based systems on network administration tasks by leveraging live network emulation to derive ground truth without human intervention. Through a full-factorial study of 24000 runs spanning 10 foundation models, 4 solver architectures, 10 task types, and 6 network topologies of increasing complexity, we show that solver design has a great impact on accuracy -- lifting a 14B open-weight model from 0.43 to 0.88 correctness -- and that such locally-deployable models can match trillion-parameter frontier systems under the right configuration. NetLLMeval is released open-source to support reproducible benchmarking of future models and solver designs.

</details>


### [10] [Maximum Achievable Burst Size in All-Optical Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27050)
*Thomas Roethig,Sleman Mouammar,Ítalo Brasileiro,Soheil Hosseini,Admela Jukan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文分析了全光卫星网络在不同星座配置下的最大突发大小，研究表明在100 Gbps上行链路容量下，基于WDM的光突发交换网络在高轨LEO星座中可支持高达500 MB的突发大小，在低轨LEO星座中可达600 MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着卫星通信需求的增长，全光卫星网络成为提高网络性能的重要方向。然而，光突发交换网络中突发大小的上限对于网络设计和性能优化至关重要，需要针对不同星座配置进行深入分析。

Method: 研究人员建立了基于波分复用(WDM)的光突发交换网络模型，在不同类型的LEO(近地轨道)星座配置下进行仿真分析，考虑了100 Gbps的上行链路容量约束，系统评估了网络支持的最大突发大小。

Result: 实验结果表明，在100 Gbps上行链路容量条件下，高轨LEO星座配置可支持最大500 MB的突发大小，而低轨LEO星座配置可支持最大600 MB的突发大小，显示出轨道高度对突发大小上限的影响。

Conclusion: 研究证明了全光卫星网络在不同星座配置下具有可观的突发传输能力，低轨LEO星座由于更短的传播距离和更低的延迟特性，相比高轨LEO星座能够支持更大的突发大小，这为卫星光网络的设计和星座选择提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We analyze the maximum burst size achievable in all-optical satellite networks across different constellations. With a 100 Gbps uplink capacity, a WDM-based optical burst switching network supports burst sizes of up to 500 MB in high-altitude LEO constellations and 600 MB in low-altitude LEO constellations.

</details>

### [11] [The Kernel's Write: Application Read-Only Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26138)
*Hui Sub Shim,Katherine Mohr,Philip Levis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出AROM机制，通过将LtRAM管理职责从硬件控制器转移到操作系统，简化DIMM硬件设计，旨在保留LtRAM密度和成本优势的同时，在读密集型工作负载上达到纯DRAM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAM的成本每比特已趋于平稳，成为数据中心增长的主要限制因素。新兴的长期RAM（LtRAM）技术提供了更密集、更廉价的主内存方案，但存在读写延迟不对称、耐久性有限和写入粒度粗糙三大缺陷。现有方案模仿闪存设备引入翻译层来隔离软件缺陷，但实验表明这会显著增加延迟。

Method: 提出应用程序只读内存（AROM）设计，将LtRAM页面设为对应用程序只读，仅在页面迁移时由操作系统写入。通过写时复制（CoW）机制强制执行：应用程序写入LtRAM时触发缺页异常，先将页面迁移回DRAM再执行存储操作。这一核心不变式使得LtRAM管理从DIMM控制器转移到操作系统，大幅简化了DIMM硬件。

Result: 目标是在读密集型工作负载上匹配纯DRAM的性能，同时保持LtRAM的密度和成本优势。

Conclusion: 通过重新设计硬件/软件接口，将更多责任卸载到操作系统，利用AROM和CoW机制简化LtRAM硬件管理，可以在读密集型场景下实现接近DRAM的性能，同时利用LtRAM的密度和成本优势。

Abstract: Alongside power, DRAM has become a major limiting factor in datacenter growth. As DRAM's cost-per-bit has plateaued over the past decade, a class of emerging memory technologies, called Long-term RAM (LtRAM), offers a path to denser and cheaper main memory.
  However, LtRAM has three main drawbacks: asymmetric read/write latencies, limited endurance, and coarse write granularity. In an attempt to isolate software from these drawbacks, LtRAM technologies such as Intel Optane copy an approach from flash devices and introduce a translation layer that manages wear-leveling, address remapping, and read/write caching. Prior experimental studies have found these operations add significantly to LtRAM latency.
  Rather than making LtRAM look like DRAM, we propose redesigning the hardware/software interface to offload more responsibility to the operating system. This design hinges on one central property, Application Read-Only Memory (AROM): LtRAM pages are read-only to applications and written only by the OS during page migrations. AROM is enforced by leveraging copy-on-write (CoW): application writes to LtRAM trigger a fault that migrates the page back to DRAM before the store is applied. This invariant allows us to shift LtRAM management from the on-DIMM controller to the operating system, drastically simplifying the DIMM's hardware. With this approach, we aim to match the performance of pure DRAM on read-mostly workloads while delivering LtRAM's density and cost advantages.

</details>


### [12] [Mitigating High-Frequency Geometric Noise in Non-Parametric 1-Bit Sparse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26137)
*Lars Kopp*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性的非参数双流形执行框架，通过硬激活阈值生成超稀疏的1-bit二进制群体编码，有效解决了重建过程中出现的高频几何噪声问题，实现了接近零的重建误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现能效高的神经形态计算，需要替代传统高功耗浮点运算的数据编码范式。现有的方法在将整数向量映射到超完备空间时会出现高频几何噪声，影响重建质量，因此需要一种有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种确定性的非参数双流形执行框架，将128元素整数向量映射到1024维超完备空间，使用8位有界变换矩阵，并强制执行硬激活阈值生成1-bit二进制群体编码。针对线性重建中的高频几何噪声，采用低开销的硬件级数字低通滤波器进行消除。

Result: 发现并解决了非参数映射中的关键表型伪影问题：低复杂度输入函数的重建误差反而高于高复杂度输入的算法复杂度悖论。通过低通滤波器完全消除了几何噪声，证明了该噪声与核心信号拓扑严格正交，将重建误差降至接近零边界。在τ=10和τ=100的分类阈值下进行了全面的实证评估验证。

Conclusion: 该架构验证了一种高度稳定、无乘法器的传统深度学习硬件替代方案，适用于边缘AI应用，具有显著的能效优势。通过硬件级数字低通滤波器有效解决了几何噪声问题，证明了该框架在实际应用中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Energy-efficient neuromorphic computing requires alternative data-encoding paradigms that bypass power-hungry floating-point operations. This paper evaluates a deterministic, non-parametric dual-manifold execution framework that maps dense 128-element integer vectors - representing digitized multi-frequency trigonometric waveforms - into a 1024-dimensional overcomplete space using 8-bit bounded transformation matrices. By enforcing a hard activation threshold, the system yields an ultra-sparse, 1-bit binary population code where y belongs to the set (0, 1)^1024. We identify and address a critical phenotypic artifact of this non-parametric mapping: the emergence of high-frequency geometric noise during linear reconstruction. Furthermore, we document an algorithmic complexity paradox where low-complexity input functions yield significantly higher reconstruction errors than highly complex, high-degree trigonometric combinations. Because the underlying basis functions operate as purely objective mathematical entities without statistical priors regarding signal smoothness, this geometric noise is proven to be strictly orthogonal to the core signal topology. Consequently, we demonstrate that a low-overhead, hardware-level digital low-pass filter completely eliminates this artifact, reducing reconstruction errors to near-zero bounds even under tight overcompleteness constraints. This architecture validates a highly stable, multiplier-free alternative to traditional deep learning hardware for edge-AI applications, verified through comprehensive empirical evaluations across varying complexity scales and classification thresholds (tau = 10 and tau = 100).

</details>


### [13] [Toward Mitigating Process-Induced Performance Degradation in 3.5D Heterogeneous Packages via Pre-Silicon Firmware Co-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26176)
*Chi Fei Chung,Nikolai Nedovodin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对英特尔3.5D异构集成封装的物理感知预测性固件调度层（XRM-SSD V24/V7.0），通过90,000步LLM推理数据集的热-电协同仿真，实现了20-30%的计算能力释放和65-68%的EDA设计裕度缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着先进3.5D封装技术的发展，热管理和功耗控制成为制约计算性能的关键瓶颈。传统的被动热管理方法难以应对动态工作负载变化，亟需开发一种能够预测并主动调度资源的方法来缓解物理限制。

Method: 采用详细的热-电协同仿真技术，基于90,000步LLM推理数据集进行分析；实现主动的工作负载密度驱动热提示机制（20-50毫秒前瞻）；扩展框架至多瓦片架构，引入N×N热耦合矩阵和双极点核；使用蒙特卡洛分析（N=2,000次试验）验证工艺变化下的鲁棒性。

Result: 热-负载相关性达R²=0.9911；补偿CPO频谱漂移控制在0.36 nm以下（仅占TSMC容差预算的21%）；HBM漏电流在所有负载状态下钳位在1 MB/hr以下；蒙特卡洛分析证实了工艺变化下的鲁棒性；预估可实现20-30%计算能力释放和65-68% EDA设计裕度缩减。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了固件-硬件协同优化在缓解先进3.5D封装物理限制方面的有效性，为未来高性能计算系统的设计优化提供了可行路径。然而，所有指标均为预硅表征的工程预测，在Intel 18A平台上的硅片验证仍待进行。

Abstract: This paper presents a pre-silicon analysis of XRM-SSD V24/V7.0, a physics-aware predictive firmware scheduling layer for Intel's 3.5D heterogeneous integrated packages (Foveros Direct 3D + PowerVia + EMIB-T + UCIe + HBM5). Using detailed thermal-electrical co-simulation over a 90,000-step LLM inference dataset, we show that proactive workload-density-driven thermal hinting (20-50 ms look-ahead) enables pre-positioning of PowerVia voltage rails. Key results include a thermal-load correlation of R^2 = 0.9911, compensated CPO spectral drift below 0.36 nm (21% of TSMC tolerance budget), and HBM leakage current clamped below 1 MB/hr across all load states. Monte Carlo analysis (N=2,000 trials) confirms robustness under process variation. V7.0 extends the framework to multi-tile architectures with an N x N thermal coupling matrix and two-pole kernel. The approach demonstrates potential for 20-30% released compute and 65-68% EDA guard-band reduction. All metrics are engineering projections from pre-silicon characterization. Silicon validation on Intel 18A platforms is pending. This work highlights firmware-hardware co-optimization as an effective approach to mitigating physical limits in advanced 3.5D packaging.

</details>


### [14] [CVA6-RT: an Open-Source Time-Predictable RV64 Processor for Mixed-Criticality Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26177)
*Enrico Zelioli,Christopher Reinwardt,Nils Wistoff,Robert Balas,Alessandro Ottaviano,Luca Benini,Angelo Garofalo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CVA6-RT通过TLB分区锁定、可重构Scratchpad缓存和低延迟中断处理等实时扩展，将中断延迟降低至12周期，达到简单微控制器水平，相比基线CVA6核心性能提升10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高性能处理器核心（如CVA6）在实时性方面存在不足，其最坏情况延迟难以预测，任务执行时序变异性较大，无法满足硬实时应用对确定性时序的严格要求。

Method: 提出CVA6-RT微架构扩展方案，包括：（1）TLB分区与锁定机制实现可预测的地址转换；（2）L1缓存动态可重构Scratchpad模式确保确定性内存访问；（3）增强型中断控制器结合硬件辅助上下文堆栈实现低延迟中断处理。

Result: 启用实时特性后，CVA6-RT的中断延迟仅为12个时钟周期，与Arm Cortex-M等简单微控制器相当，相比基线CVA6核心降低了一个数量级（10倍）。

Conclusion: CVA6-RT成功将高性能RISC-V核心扩展为具备实时能力的处理器，在保持rv64gch指令集架构的同时，实现了与简单实时微控制器相当的确定性时序性能，填补了高性能与实时性之间的空白。

Abstract: This work presents CVA6-RT, a real-time micro-architectural extension of the CVA6 core to bound worst-case latency and reduce task's timing execution variability. CVA6-RT implements the rv64gch ISA and features advanced support for real-time execution, including TLB partitioning and locking for predictable address translation, a dynamically reconfigurable scratchpad mode in the L1 caches for deterministic memory access, and low-latency interrupt handling via an enhanced interrupt controller combined with hardware-assisted context stacking. With real-time features enabled, CVA6-RT achieves an interrupt latency of 12 cycles, comparable to that of simpler Arm Cortex-M microcontrollers, and 10x lower than the baseline CVA6 core.

</details>


### [15] [Nanoelectromechanical Systems (NEMS) for Hardware Security in Advanced Packaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26426)
*Himanandhan Reddy Kottur,Pavanbabu Arjunamahanthi,M. Shafkat M. Khan,Liton Kumar Biswas,Nitin Varshney,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出将纳米机电系统（NEMS）作为新兴硬件安全原语，利用其物理不可克隆函数、形状记忆材料和共振指纹等机制，提供抗逆向工程和侧信道攻击的设备级安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着半导体制造和先进封装领域的硬件安全威胁不断升级，针对篡改、伪造和供应链渗透等复杂攻击，亟需新型物理机制来增强硬件安全性。

Method: 采用NEMS基物理不可克隆函数（PUFs）、形状记忆材料、共振指纹和物理解锁架构，利用机械不可预测性和制造诱导的纳米尺度变异性构建安全机制。

Result: NEMS技术相比传统数字安全方法展现出对逆向工程、侧信道攻击和环境退化更强的抵御能力，同时具备低功耗特性和物理鲁棒性。

Conclusion: NEMS技术可无缝集成到标准半导体工艺流程中，为国防、航空航天、关键基础设施和消费电子领域提供可扩展、可验证且安全的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: As hardware security threats escalate across semiconductor manufacturing and advanced packaging, there is a growing need for novel physical mechanisms to counter sophisticated attacks such as tampering, counterfeiting, and supply chain infiltration. This paper presents Nanoelectromechanical Systems (NEMS) as an emerging class of hardware security primitives that enable physical assurance, tamper detection, and authentication at the device level. Leveraging mechanisms such as NEMS-based Physically Unclonable Functions (PUFs), shape memory materials, resonance-based fingerprints, and physical unlocking architectures, these systems offer enhanced resilience to reverse engineering, side-channel attacks, and environmental degradation. By harnessing mechanical unpredictability and fabrication-induced nanoscale variability, NEMS technologies introduce a physically robust and low-power alternative to conventional digital security methods. Their seamless integration into standard semiconductor workflows paves the way for scalable, verifiable, and secure solutions across defense, aerospace, critical infrastructure, and consumer electronics.

</details>


### [16] [GRAINS: Storage-Aware Algorithm-Architecture Co-Design Enabling High-Performance and Low-Cost Graph-Based Genome Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26468)
*Nika Mansouri Ghiasi,Harun Mustafa,Talu Güloglu,Rakesh Nadig,Konstantina Koliogeorgi,Susana Rebolledo Ruiz,Marc Rautmann,Furkan Eris,Mohammad Sadrosadati,Jisung Park,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: GRAINS是首个支持大规模基因组图分析的在存储系统内处理方案，通过存储感知的算法-架构协同设计，相比软件基线实现2.7x-47.8x加速和4.4x-31.6x能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组图能高效表示大规模基因组数据库，但在分析时会产生大量低复用数据的访问，导致存储系统数据移动开销显著。现有工具无法在现代SSD有限的硬件资源内高效运行，且传统线性基因组分析或常规图分析的在存储系统方案不适用于基因组图的独特数据结构和访问模式。

Method: 采用存储感知的算法-架构协同设计，包含三个关键方面：(1) 基于基因组图独特特征提出新的批处理和执行流程；(2) 通过闪存内和存储内处理避免传输低复用闪存页面；(3) 设计轻量级调度技术利用闪存裸片的全部并行性，该技术通过重新利用现有SSD结构实现。

Result: 相比最先进的软件基线实现2.7x-47.8x加速（能耗降低4.4x-31.6x），相比硬件加速基线实现1.5x-17.0x加速（能耗降低3.1x-20.7x）。

Conclusion: GRAINS证明了通过存储感知的协同设计，可以有效地解决大规模基因组图分析中的数据移动瓶颈问题，在性能和能效方面均获得显著提升。

Abstract: Graph-based representations of genome sequences have emerged as a powerful approach for representing massive genomic databases in an expressive and efficient way. Despite their benefits, analysis on large-scale genome graphs incurs significant data movement overhead from the storage system due to accessing large amounts of low-reuse data. Processing data directly inside the storage device can be a fundamental solution for mitigating this overhead. However, none of the existing tools for graph-based genome analysis can be efficiently used inside the storage system due to the limited internal hardware resources in modern SSDs. At the same time, prior storage-centric systems developed for (i) traditional, linear non-graph-based genome analysis or (ii) conventional, non-genomic graph analysis are not suitable for the unique data structures and access patterns of graph-based genome analysis.
  We propose GRAINS, the first system for analysis with large-scale genome graphs in storage. Through our detailed examination of typical analysis pipelines that operate on genome graphs, we perform storage-aware algorithm-architecture co-design to (i) make these pipelines more storage-friendly and (ii) further improve performance, energy-efficiency, and cost via in-storage and in-flash processing. GRAINS's co-design is based on three key aspects. First, we propose a new batching and execution flow, based on unique features of genome graphs. Second, via in-flash and in-storage processing, we avoid transferring low-reused flash pages. Third, to leverage the full parallelism of flash dies, we design an effective, yet lightweight, scheduling technique, enabled by re-purposing the existing SSD structures. GRAINS provides 2.7x-47.8x speedup (4.4x-31.6x energy reduction) over the state-of-the-art software baselines, and 1.5x-17.0x speedup (3.1x-20.7x energy reduction) over a hardware-accelerated baseline.

</details>


### [17] [SegFold: Accelerating Sparse GEMM with a Fine-Grained Dynamic Dataflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26701)
*Xinrui Wu,Hanyu Wang,Jason Cong,Tony Nowatzki*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Segment的动态数据流，并通过协同设计的SpGEMM加速器SegFold实现。相比最先进的加速器实现了1.95倍的加速，相比最佳静态数据流配置实现了5.3倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SpGEMM加速器采用静态数据流（如内积、外积、Gustavson等），这些静态方法牺牲了数据复用机会并限制了负载均衡。为了解决这一低效问题，需要引入动态性来优化数据复用和负载均衡。

Method: 提出了Segment数据流，包含两个关键创新：1）细粒度动态调度以优化复用并减少资源竞争；2）动态重映射部分完成的工作以改善负载均衡。设计了SegFold加速器，包含能够识别局部窗口内细粒度复用机会的内存控制器，以及能够动态路由输入和重映射工作的合并网络。

Result: 在不同密度和矩阵大小下，SegFold相比最先进的SpGEMM加速器实现了几何平均1.95倍加速，相比最佳静态数据流配置实现了5.3倍加速。证明了在数据流设计空间中添加动态性可以释放静态调度无法单独实现的复用和负载均衡增益。

Conclusion: 通过在数据流设计中引入动态性，可以解锁静态调度方法无法实现的数据复用和负载平衡优化，这为SpGEMM加速器设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Generalized sparse matrix-matrix multiplication (SpGEMM) is critical in many domains. Existing CPUs and GPUs, as well as specialized accelerators, rely on static dataflows (e.g., inner product, outer product, Gustavson, etc.). Each static dataflow sacrifices some data reuse opportunities and imposes constraints on load balance.
  To address this inefficiency, we extend the typical SpGEMM dataflows by considering dynamism. Specifically, we add fine-grained dynamic scheduling to optimize reuse and reduce resource contention. We also develop dynamic remapping of partially completed work to improve load balance and parallelism. These ideas are formalized into a specific dataflow called Segment. To demonstrate Segment, we codesign a SpGEMM accelerator called SegFold. SegFold includes a memory controller that identifies fine-grained reuse opportunities in a local window of the stationary input array and exploits them through dynamic work assignment. It also incorporates a merge network that routes inputs to appropriate processing elements (PEs) for computation while dynamically remapping the work assigned to each PE to balance load.
  Across diverse densities and matrix sizes, SegFold achieves a geometric-mean $1.95\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art SpGEMM accelerators and $5.3\times$ over the best static dataflow configuration, demonstrating that adding dynamism to the dataflow design space unlocks reuse and load-balance gains that no static scheduling choice can achieve in isolation.

</details>

*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文在Apple M4 Pro上表征了GPU命令完成后CPU-GPU统一内存的缓存状态边界，发现大足迹GPU内核会导致可复现的共享缓存驱逐，并量化了一种简单的软件恢复机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Silicon提供统一的CPU-GPU内存，但GPU命令完成后的缓存状态未文档化。开发者无法确定GPU操作结束后CPU访问内存时的性能影响，这阻碍了对性能关键应用的优化。

Method: 在14核Apple M4 Pro上：1) 使用STREAM和BabelStream验证测量流程；2) 设计同步Metal实验，GPU内核访问0-512 MiB内存后，CPU进行16 MiB探测；3) 通过匹配块实验测量高优先级CPU流量下的GPU减速；4) 利用PMU和IOReport直方图提供硬件层面的数据支撑。

Result: GPU大足迹操作后的首次CPU遍历明显变慢，第二次遍历消除大部分开销，证明是残留共享缓存驱逐而非DRAM竞争。识别出M4 Pro上可复现的后GPU缓存驱逐窗口，并量化了单遍软件恢复机制的有效性。硬件测量揭示了L1D重填扇区与软件缓存行大小的差异、页偏移相关的冲突行为，以及性能核、能效核和AGX的需求分离。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统性地表征了Apple Silicon上GPU命令完成后的缓存状态边界，为统一内存编程模型提供了重要的性能洞察，提出的软件恢复机制可直接指导实际应用的性能优化。

Abstract: Apple silicon exposes unified CPU-GPU memory, but the cache state left after a completed GPU command is not documented. This paper characterizes that phase boundary on a 14-core Apple M4 Pro. We validate the measurement pipeline against unmodified STREAM 5.10 and BabelStream 5.0, then adapt an 8192-byte system-level-cache occupancy pattern to a synchronized Metal experiment. A GPU kernel touches 0 to 512 MiB and finishes before a 16 MiB CPU probe begins. The first CPU traversal is slower after large GPU footprints, while a second traversal removes most of the cost, showing residual shared-cache displacement rather than simultaneous DRAM contention. A separate matched-block experiment measures GPU slowdown under high-priority CPU traffic and finds background QoS close to baseline. Root PMU measurements and public IOReport histograms provide hardware grounding: they distinguish L1D refill sectors from software cache-line size, expose page-offset-dependent conflict behavior, and separate performance-core, efficiency-core, and AGX demand. The results identify a reproducible post-GPU cache-displacement window on M4 Pro and quantify a simple one-pass software recovery mechanism.

</details>


### [19] [Evaluating Architectural Trade-offs in CGRAs: The Impact of Scratchpad Memory and Heterogeneity on Compute-Intensive Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27240)
*María José Belda,Lara Orlandic,Fernando Castro,Miguel Peón-Quirós,Katzalin Olcoz,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了CGRA架构中处理单元异构性与本地数据复用支持的影响。研究发现，带有暂存器存储器(SPM)的异构架构能将内存流量减少8倍，在数据混洗任务中能效更优；而同构架构面积开销减少4.4-8.2倍，工作频率达700MHz，矩阵计算速度提升高达5倍。为边缘计算场景下的CGRA架构选择提供了设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代边缘计算应用，特别是Vision Transformers(ViTs)等高吞吐量流处理任务，在严格的功耗和面积约束下需要大规模空间并行性和高效数据移动。CGRAs作为平衡性能、灵活性和能效的有前途的范式，其关键设计选择(处理单元异构性和本地数据复用支持)的影响需要深入分析。

Method: 评估了FFT和GEMM等核心计算内核以及端到端癫痫检测Transformer工作负载。对比了两种配置：基准同构架构和集成了专用功能单元与暂存器存储器(SPM)的异构演进架构。通过分析数据移动优化、面积开销和执行频率等指标进行系统评估。

Result: SPM显著优化数据移动，相比无内存设计将内存流量减少8倍。异构架构在数据混洗任务中实现更优能效；同构设计相比最先进CGRAs将面积开销减少4.4-8.2倍，并维持700MHz工作频率，在矩阵计算中相比异构配置实现高达5倍执行加速。

Conclusion: 为基于算术强度、性能目标和资源约束来选择边缘级工作负载的CGRA架构提供了架构路线图，帮助设计者根据具体应用需求在异构性与同构性之间做出合理选择。

Abstract: Modern edge computing applications, particularly high-throughput stream processing like Vision Transformers (ViTs), demand massive spatial parallelism and efficient data movement under tight power and area constraints. Coarse-Grained Reconfigurable Architectures (CGRAs) offer a promising paradigm to balance performance, flexibility, and energy efficiency. This paper analyzes the impact of two critical CGRA design choices: processing element heterogeneity and local data reuse support. We evaluate essential computational kernels (Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) and General Matrix Multiply (GEMM)) alongside an end-to-end seizure detection transformer workload across two distinct configurations: a baseline homogeneous architecture and a heterogeneous evolution integrating specialized functional units with an Scratchpad Memory (SPM). Our evaluation demonstrates that the SPM significantly optimizes data movement, reducing memory traffic eightfold compared to a memory-less design. While the heterogeneous architecture achieves superior energy efficiency for data-shuffling tasks, the homogeneous design minimizes area overhead by 4.4x to 8.2x relative to state-of-the-art CGRAs. Furthermore, it sustains a 700 MHz operating frequency, enabling up to a 5x execution speedup over the heterogeneous configuration during matrix computations. Ultimately, this work provides an architectural roadmap for selecting CGRA fabrics based on the arithmetic intensity, performance goals, and resource envelopes of edge-scale workloads.

</details>


### [20] [CHIA: An open-source framework for principled, agentic AI-driven hardware/software co-design research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27350)
*Angela Cui,Ferran Hermida-Rivera,Jack Toubes,Raghav Gupta,Jim Fang,Chengyi Lux Zhang,Ella Schwarz,Junha Kim,Yakun Sophia Shao,Borivoje Nikolic,Christopher W. Fletcher,Sagar Karandikar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHIA，一个开源的硬件/软件协同设计框架，用于支持AI驱动的系统设计研究。CHIA通过“CHIA loops”表示设计流程，提供丰富的节点库和大规模执行能力，并通过五个案例展示了其在架构设计、RTL实现和自动化维护方面的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在硬件/软件协同设计中展现出巨大潜力，但现有应用多局限于小规模、隔离场景，缺乏能够支持复杂AI驱动设计流程的统一框架。设计、部署和管理此类流程的困难阻碍了AI在该领域的广泛应用与科学研究。因此，需要一个开源、可扩展且支持科学验证的框架来推动这一研究方向。

Method: 提出CHIA框架，核心方法包括：（1）将AI驱动的硬件/软件设计流程表示为“CHIA loops”，即有向循环图，节点执行各类SoC设计工具、模拟器、编译系统、AI模型和进化编码代理等；（2）提供丰富的节点库，支持Chipyard、gem5、ChampSim、FireSim、Hammer、Vivado、AlphaEvolve、AdaEvolve等主流工具；（3）实现AI模型与硬件工具的隔离、性能分析、容错执行，以及跨异构系统（CPU、FPGA、GPU，公有云/本地）的大规模可靠执行能力。

Result: 通过五个案例研究展示了CHIA的有效性：（1）RTL到gem5模拟器的自动对齐；（2）LLM驱动的RTL微架构特征实现；（3）代理式IPC感知关键路径优化；（4）进化式架构发现；（5）维护友好的代理式GitHub问题修复。这些案例证明了CHIA在自动化设计流程、提升设计效率和实现科学验证方面的能力。

Conclusion: CHIA为AI驱动的硬件/软件协同设计研究提供了一个开源、可扩展且支持科学验证的框架。通过CHIA loops的抽象和丰富的节点库，研究者能够更高效地构建和部署复杂的设计流程，推动该领域的创新和科学研究。五个案例研究验证了框架的实用性和潜力，为未来的研究方向提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence shows great promise for radically improving the pace of innovation in hardware/software co-design research across computer architecture, systems, compilers, and VLSI. Thus far, however, applications of AI in these contexts have generally been demonstrated in isolated settings on small-scale problems, due to the difficulty of designing and deploying complex AI-infused hardware and software development workflows.
  This paper introduces CHIA, an open-source hardware/software co-design framework for agile and principled research on the application of AI to co-design. CHIA treats the productive construction and scalable deployment of the co-design flow itself as a first-class objective. In CHIA, agentic AI-driven hardware and software design flows are expressed as \textit{CHIA loops}: directed cyclic graphs whose nodes execute various system-on-chip design tools, microarchitectural simulators, software build systems, AI models, evolutionary coding agents, and more. The \textit{CHIA library} provides node implementations for many popular tools, including Chipyard, gem5, ChampSim, FireSim, Hammer (thus several commercial ASIC CAD tools), Vivado, AlphaEvolve, AdaEvolve, and many others.
  CHIA also provides a broad set of features to conduct principled science around these flows. These include isolation between AI models and hardware tools, profiling mechanisms, fault-tolerant execution, and reliability at scale across hundreds of heterogeneous systems (CPUs, FPGAs, GPUs, etc., across public cloud/on-prem.).
  To showcase CHIA, we present five CHIA loops as case studies: (1) automatic RTL-to-gem5 simulator alignment, (2) LLM-driven implementation of microarchitectural features in RTL, (3) agentic, IPC-aware critical path optimization, (4) evolutionary architectural discovery, and (5) maintainer-friendly agentic GitHub issue fixing.

</details>

### [21] [Hot AI in Cold Space: Thermal-Crosstalk-Aware Scheduling for Sustainable Orbital AI Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26150)
*Shuyi Chen,Zhengchang Hua,Nikos Tziritas,Georgios Theodoropoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对轨道数据中心(LLM训练)面临的"邻近-热悖论"(高密度导致热串扰、散热困难)，提出热感知异构论和热负载均衡(TLB)框架，通过动态迁移工作负载至最冷却单元，恢复模型算力利用率并延长硬件寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面AI训练能耗与水资源消耗不可持续，轨道数据中心(ODCs)作为"零运营碳"替代方案面临极端物理密度要求导致的"邻近-热悖论":高密度结构中的热-流耦合串扰(共享冷却回路热陷阱)和热-辐射串扰(相互加热阻挡深空散热器)引发热节流与热疲劳，严重降低训练吞吐量并缩短硬件寿命。

Method: 提出热感知异构性论题，将空间冷却差异作为主要资源管理维度；构建热负载均衡(TLB)软件框架，基于实时流体温度或吸收辐射动态迁移LLM工作负载至最冷却可用单元。

Result: TLB有效缓解热瓶颈，恢复模型浮点运算利用率(MFU)，同时降低物理热应力，延长轨道硬件运行寿命，有助于分摊火箭发射的巨额隐含碳排放。

Conclusion: 延长轨道硬件使用寿命对摊销火箭发射的大量隐含碳至关重要，为在不加速太空电子废弃物的前提下扩展轨道AI提供了必要路径。

Abstract: Terrestrial AI training faces an unsustainable energy and water crisis, positioning Orbital Data Centers (ODCs) as a "zero operational carbon" alternative. However, the sub-$10μ\text{s}$ communication latency required for distributed Large Language Model (LLM) training forces ODCs into extreme physical density, triggering a critical "Proximity-Thermal Paradox." As these high-density systems scale into Monolithic Structures or Proximity Swarms, they suffer from intense thermal-fluid crosstalk (heat traps in shared cooling loops) and thermal-radiative crosstalk (mutual heating that blocks deep-space cooling radiators). If left unmitigated, this persistent heat stagnation not only triggers severe thermal throttling that degrades training throughput, but also induces severe thermal fatigue, drastically shortening hardware lifespans and generating premature space e-waste. To make orbital AI truly sustainable, this position paper challenges traditional uniform load-sharing. We propose the Thermal-Aware Heterogeneity Thesis, which treats spatial cooling variances as a primary resource management dimension. Building on this, we introduce Thermal-Load Balancing (TLB), a software framework that dynamically migrates LLM workloads to the coolest available units based on instantaneous fluid temperatures or absorbed radiation. Our analysis demonstrates that TLB resolves thermal bottlenecks to restore Model Flops Utilization (MFU), while simultaneously reducing physical thermal stress. Extending the operational lifespan of orbital hardware is crucial to amortize the massive embodied carbon of rocket launches, outlining a necessary pathway to scale orbital AI without accelerating e-waste.

</details>


### [22] [FinWhale: An Optimally Resilient Two-Round Terminating DAG Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26292)
*Razya Ladelsky,Roy Friedman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了FinWhale，首个支持两消息延迟快速路径的DAG拜占庭容错协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DAG拜占庭容错协议在高吞吐量下仍需至少三消息延迟才能提交决策，而快速路径协议虽能在有利条件下实现最优两消息延迟终止，却难以与DAG结构兼容。

Method: FinWhale通过扩展Mysticeti协议，设计了新颖的快速路径提交机制，使其与原有慢速路径规则安全共存；并引入基于快速路径证据区块的新提交结构，确保验证者跨不同本地DAG视图时安全一致地融合两种路径推理。

Result: FinWhale在部分同步模型下运行，采用n = 3f + 2p - 1的验证者数量配置，达到快速拜占庭共识的已知下界；容忍最多f个拜占庭故障，在快速路径期间最多p个验证者失败时实现快速终止（1 ≤ p ≤ f）。实验结果表明最优延迟快速路径可成功集成至无证书DAG共识协议中。

Conclusion: 研究表明最优延迟的快速路径可以有效地集成到无证书DAG共识协议中，为DAG拜占庭容错协议的延迟优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: DAG based Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocols provide high throughput consensus under partial synchrony but existing DAG protocols still require at least three message delays to commit decisions. In contrast fast path Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocols can achieve optimal two message delay termination under favorable conditions though they do not naturally extend to DAGs.
  We present FinWhale the first DAG based Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocol with a two message delay fast path. FinWhale extends Mysticeti with a novel fast path commit mechanism that safely coexists with the protocol's original slow path rules. To preserve safety across different local DAG views we introduce new commit structures based on fast path evidence blocks enabling validators to combine fast path and slow path reasoning consistently.
  FinWhale operates in the partially synchronous model with n equals three f plus two p minus one validators matching the known lower bound for fast Byzantine consensus. The protocol tolerates up to f Byzantine faults and achieves fast termination whenever at most p validators fail during the fast path where p is between one and f. Our results show that optimal latency fast paths can be integrated into uncertified DAG consensus protocols.

</details>


### [23] [A Distributed Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm Simulator for Engineering Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26297)
*Ali Rajabi,Milad Hasanzadeh,Amin Kargarian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个与Qiskit兼容的分布式量子近似优化算法（DQAOA）开源模拟器，用于解决工程设计和决策应用中的二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，支持单体和分布式QAOA模式，并提供图形界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对工程设计和决策应用中QUBO问题的求解需求，现有缺乏可重用的模拟器来比较不同QAOA执行模式的性能。需要一个能够支持单体和分布式QAOA、便于用户配置和比较QUBO实例求解结果的工具。

Method: 开发了基于Qiskit的DQAOA模拟器，支持单体QAOA（单QPU）和分布式QAOA（多QPU）两种执行模式。工作流程包括：QUBO模型规范化、映射到代价哈密顿量、跨QPU变量分配、识别局部和跨QPU耦合、构建相应电路。实现了运行时优化（参数化电路重用、固定深度目标重用、批量评估、并行多起点执行）和Streamlit图形用户界面。

Result: 在独立QUBO基准测试和发电机组组合应用案例中验证了模拟器的有效性。在机组组合案例中，暴力搜索、单体QAOA和分布式QAOA均得到相同的组合比特串和运行成本。多案例研究表明模拟器产生与经典单体QAOA参考一致的最优比特串和成本。分阶段运行时分析显示实现阶段有显著运行时减少，但分布式QAOA由于跨QPU耦合需要远程操作仍更耗时。

Conclusion: 该开源DQAOA模拟器成功填补了可重用QUBO求解工具的空白，为比较不同QAOA执行模式提供了有效平台，在保持求解质量的同时实现了运行时优化，为工程设计和决策应用中的量子优化算法应用提供了实用工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a Qiskit-compatible distributed quantum approximate optimization algorithm (DQAOA) simulator for quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problems arising in engineering design and decision applications. The open-source simulator is available through the RAISE LAB website and GitHub repository, with README documentation for installation, input formatting, configurable parameters, and example workflows. The package addresses the need for a reusable simulator that can solve and compare QUBO instances across different QAOA execution modes. It supports monolithic QAOA on a single quantum processing unit (QPU) and distributed QAOA across a user-specified number of QPUs with configurable capacities. The workflow canonicalizes the QUBO model, maps it to a cost Hamiltonian, allocates variables across QPUs, identifies local and cross-QPU couplings, and constructs the corresponding circuits. Runtime optimizations, including parameterized circuit reuse, objective reuse at fixed depth, batched evaluations, and parallel multi-start execution, reduce repeated overhead. A Streamlit graphical user interface is also provided for entering or uploading QUBO instances, configuring solver settings, running selected modes, and visualizing solution-quality metrics without editing Python scripts. The package is demonstrated on standalone QUBO benchmarks and a power generation unit commitment application. In the unit commitment case, brute force, monolithic QAOA, and distributed QAOA recover the same commitment bitstring and operating cost. Across multiple case studies, the simulator produces results consistent with classical monolithic QAOA references in terms of optimal bitstrings and costs. Staged runtime analysis shows substantial runtime reduction across implementation stages, while distributed QAOA remains more demanding because cross-QPU couplings require remote operations.

</details>


### [24] [Priceless: An examination of Serverless Functions-as-a-Service (FaaS) pricing models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26308)
*Nnamdi Ekwe-Ekwe*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Priceless工具，对AWS Lambda、Microsoft Azure Functions和Google Cloud Functions三大FaaS平台的功能和定价模型进行详细分析与比较，发现AWS整体成本最低，Azure最昂贵，且存在显著的跨提供商和跨区域成本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无服务器函数即服务（FaaS）在过去十年的发展，各提供商推出了众多新功能，同时也带来了多样化且复杂的定价模型，这些定价模型在不同提供商之间存在差异，缺乏系统性的比较分析，用户难以做出最优的成本选择。

Method: 提出Priceless方法，系统性地审查当前FaaS平台的功能及其定价模型，然后选择示例工作负载，在AWS Lambda、Microsoft Azure Functions和Google Cloud Functions三个主要平台上运行，进行跨提供商和跨区域的比较价格分析。

Result: 发现了显著的跨提供商成本差异，AWS是运行函数成本最低的平台，Microsoft Azure在等效工作负载下最为昂贵；同时还发现了同一提供商内部不同区域之间存在显著的成本差异。

Conclusion: FaaS平台的定价模型复杂且存在显著差异，用户在选择提供商和部署区域时需要仔细考虑成本因素，AWS在成本效益方面具有优势，而跨区域部署也会对成本产生重要影响。

Abstract: Serverless Functions-as-a-Service providers have grown in their offering since inception a decade ago, with a myriad of new functionalities offered to end-users. These new features have also brought new, varied and at times complex pricing models that differ between providers. In this paper, we present Priceless, a detailed examination of the current state of the art of FaaS features, and their pricing models. We then perform a comparative price analysis of running example workloads across AWS Lambda, Microsoft Azure Functions and Google Cloud Functions. Our work finds significant cost differences both cross-provider, but also, cross-region within the provider. We find that AWS is the cheapest overall to run functions on, with Microsoft Azure being the most expensive for equivalent workloads.

</details>


### [25] [Moebius: Serving Mixture-of-Expert Models with Seamless Runtime Parallelism Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26607)
*Shaoyu Wang,Yizhuo Liang,Jaeyong Song,Chong Li,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Moebius是一个MoE模型服务系统，能够在运行时动态切换专家并行（EP）和张量并行（TP），在所有工作负载下匹配最优静态方案，RL任务中性能提升1.16-1.25倍，切换开销215-434ms，内存开销仅2.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型服务需要多GPU并行（TP或EP），但最优选择依赖并发请求数：TP适合低并发，EP适合高并发。实际生产负载并发度波动剧烈（在线服务突发流量、RL rollouts），固定任一并行策略都会在跨边界时损失性能。

Method: 提出Moebius系统，基于关键洞察——EP和TP是同一模型的两种布局，计算相同函数且权重/KV缓存字节级相同，切换只需重分配各rank拥有的切片所有权。利用高带宽GPU互联，在解码步骤间完成重分片，无需重启引擎或丢弃进行中的请求。保持两种并行运行时常驻，对单份专家权重和KV缓存在固定地址进行重分片，使用融合GPU-to-GPU传输内核。

Result: 在8x H200 GPU上服务Qwen3-235B-A22B模型：Moebius在每个操作点匹配较优的静态并行策略；RL rollouts中超越最优静态方案1.16-1.25倍；每次切换耗时215-434ms；双布局常驻仅需2.4%内存开销。

Conclusion: Moebius成功实现了MoE模型服务中EP与TP的运行时动态切换，有效解决了负载波动下的并行策略选择问题，在保持低内存开销的同时获得全场景最优性能，为实际生产环境中的MoE服务提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) to hundreds of billions of parameters. Serving a single MoE model requires multiple GPUs operating in parallel, typically through tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP). The optimal choice depends on the number of in-flight requests: TP is faster at low concurrency, whereas EP wins at high concurrency. Production workloads cross this boundary continually: online serving sees bursty arrivals that subside into quiet periods, and reinforcement-learning rollouts begin as a high-concurrency burst that decays into a long tail of stragglers. Pinning either layout therefore forfeits performance when the workload crosses to the other side.
  We present Moebius, a serving system that switches between EP and TP at runtime without restarting the engine or dropping in-flight requests. Our key insight is that EP and TP are two layouts of one model, not two models: they compute the same function over byte-identical expert weights and KV cache, so a switch changes only which rank owns each slice. Moving those owner-changed slices is the sole irreducible cost, and modern high-bandwidth GPU interconnects make it fast enough to do between decode steps without draining in-flight requests. Moebius preserves each parallelism's runtime resident, and reshards the single copy of expert weights and KV cache at fixed addresses with fused GPU-to-GPU transfer kernels. On 8x H200 GPUs serving Qwen3-235B-A22B, Moebius matches the better static parallelism at every operating point, and beats it on RL rollouts by 1.16-1.25x across steps. Each switch completes in 215-434 ms, and Moebius holds both layouts resident with only 2.4% memory overhead.

</details>


### [26] [Simulating Unified Tensor Resharding in heterogeneous AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26633)
*Sumit Kumar,Sayantan Dasgupta,Kushal Mitra,Meet Dadhania,Rohan Sudhir Basugade,Praveen Tammana,Satananda Burla,Abed Mohammad Kamaluddin,Rinku Shah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Xsim，一个面向分布式大语言模型训练的异构感知模拟器，通过非均匀负载划分和异构感知的集合通信等关键特性，在真实异构部署场景下实现了高精度（误差<5%）的训练时间预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最先进AI训练模拟器假设基础设施是同构的，但现实世界的训练基础设施正变得越来越异构化，原因包括：(a) 多模态和MoE等模型架构利用异构性来提高设备利用率；(b) 公有云平台由于硬件快速迭代，同构硬件的可用性有限；(c) 大型企业通常部署地理分布式的异构基础设施。

Method: Xsim提出了五项核心技术：(i) 通过跨异构设备组的非均匀工作负载划分实现负载均衡；(ii) 通过自定义环形构建和块分区实现异构感知的集合通信；(iii) 为新兴流水线并行算法和非均匀张量重分片技术提供可复用的异构感知抽象；(iv) 提供灵活的输入抽象，支持自定义设备组和设备到并行策略的映射；(v) 支持与NS-3和htsim的可插拔集成，允许用户在模拟保真度与性能/可扩展性之间进行权衡。

Result: 实验评估表明，Xsim能够准确预测真实世界异构部署的训练时间：在大多数异构数据并行/张量并行配置下误差小于5%，在流水线并行通信建模中误差约为2%。此外，系统还能暴露流水线气泡时间和落后者等待时间等可操作的指标。

Conclusion: Xsim成功填补了当前AI训练模拟器在异构基础设施支持方面的空白，为异构环境下的分布式LLM训练提供了高精度、可扩展的模拟解决方案，能够帮助用户优化训练部署策略。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI training simulators assume homogeneous compute and network infrastructure. However, real-world training infrastructure is becoming increasingly heterogeneous since: (a) Model architectures such as multimodal and MoE exploit heterogeneity to improve device utilization, (b) Public cloud platforms often provide limited availability of homogeneous hardware due to fast hardware evolution, and (c) Large enterprises frequently deploy geographically distributed infrastructure that is both diverse and heterogeneous. In this paper, we present Xsim, a heterogeneity-aware simulator for distributed LLM training. Xsim supports: (i) Load balancing through non-uniform workload partitioning across heterogeneous device groups, (ii) Heterogeneity-aware collective communication via customized ring construction and chunk partitioning, (iii) Reusable heterogeneity-aware abstractions for emerging pipeline-parallel algorithms and non-uniform tensor resharding technique, (iv) Flexible input abstractions for specifying deployment plans with custom device groups and custom device-to-parallelism mappings, and (v) Pluggable integration with NS-3 and htsim, allowing users to trade off simulation fidelity for performance and scalability. Our evaluation demonstrates that Xsim accurately predicts training time for real-world heterogeneous deployments, with an error of less than 5% across most heterogeneous data-parallel/tensor-parallel configurations and around 2% error with pipeline-parallel communication modeling. We expose actionable metrics such as pipeline bubble time and straggler waiting time.

</details>


### [27] [RolloutPipe: Overlapping Pipelined Rollout and Training in Disaggregated On-Policy LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26997)
*Rongjian Chen,Jianmin Hu,Kejiang Ye,Minxian Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了RolloutPipe框架，通过完全组流水线和前沿组调度技术，解决了分布式RLVR系统中训练器闲置问题，显著缩短了30.7%-42.3%的训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的同步策略GRPO RLVR系统在整个rollout完成后才开始训练，导致训练器GPU池在rollout期间处于闲置状态；而异步RL流水线虽然重叠了两个阶段，但代价是使用陈旧数据进行训练，影响训练质量。

Method: 提出RolloutPipe框架，包含两个核心技术：（1）完全组流水线（CGP）：在组实现完成后立即将可训练的完整组分派到训练器FIFO队列；（2）前沿组调度（FGD）：在Rollout节点上的准入策略，优先处理形成下一个训练批所需的前沿组请求，使训练器就绪组更早、更稳定到达。这种设计在rollout完成前就开始训练，同时保持在线策略的正确性。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B模型上，跨越四个推理和科学基准测试以及十二种rollout设置进行评估，RolloutPipe将rollout到训练结束的时间缩短了30.7%-42.3%，与最先进的Slime系统相比，将训练器等待比率降低了37%-76%。

Conclusion: RolloutPipe成功地在分布式RLVR系统中实现了训练与rollout的有效重叠，既解决了训练器闲置问题，又保持了在线策略训练的正确性，为大规模语言模型推理后训练提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training for reasoning increasingly relies on reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where models learn from ground-truth feedback on mathematical, logical, and scientific tasks. To enable flexible resource allocation and support heterogeneous training setups, modern RLVR systems adopt disaggregated architectures that decouple rollout generation and policy training across independent GPU pools. However, existing synchronous on-policy GRPO (Group Relative Policy Optimization) RLVR systems finish an entire rollout before starting training, leaving the trainer GPU pool idle while rollout is still ongoing. Asynchronous RL pipelines overlap the two stages, but at the cost of training on stale data. To address these challenges, we propose RolloutPipe, a post-training framework for disaggregated RLVR systems, which turns the fixed-weight rollout into a complete-group pipeline where trainable groups move to the trainer while later groups are still being generated. RolloutPipe achieves this through two techniques including complete-group pipelining (CGP) and frontier-group dispatch (FGD). CGP dispatches each trainable complete group to the trainer FIFO as soon as group materialization finishes, and FGD is an admission policy on the Rollout node that first admits requests for the frontier groups needed to form the next training batch, so that trainer-ready groups arrive earlier and more steadily. The design starts training before the rollout completes while maintaining on-policy correctness. Evaluated on Qwen3-1.7B across four reasoning and science benchmarks and twelve rollout settings, RolloutPipe shortens the rollout-to-train-end time by 30.7%-42.3%, and lowers the trainer waiting ratio by 37%-76% compared to Slime, a state-of-the-art rollout and training system.

</details>


### [28] [DMuon: Efficient Distributed Muon Training with Near-Adam Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27153)
*Vincent Chen,Starrick Liu,Regis Cheng,Dance Yang,Shalfun Li,Ryan Yu,Lucy Liang,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DMuon，一种开源的分布式Muon优化器实现，通过优化矩阵级优化器的计算开销，在深度学习训练中实现了显著的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式训练基础设施基于元素级优化器设计，无法有效支持Muon等矩阵级优化器。Muon需要耦合整个权重矩阵并进行昂贵的Newton-Schulz迭代，导致普通实现的计算成本超过前向和反向传播的两倍，这限制了其在实际训练中的应用。

Method: 提出了DMuon，一种开源的分布式Muon实现，作为即插即用模块集成到现有训练流程中，无需框架级修改。该方法针对矩阵级优化器的特性优化了分布式训练基础设施的设计。

Result: 在具身基础模型和大语言模型（LLM）训练任务中，DMuon在端到端步骤时间上实现了1.48x-3.01x加速，在优化器步骤时间上实现了6.85x-163.00x加速，将每步延迟降低到接近AdamW的水平，实现了高效的模型训练扩展。

Conclusion: DMuon成功弥合了矩阵级优化器与现有分布式训练基础设施之间的差距，使Mu等矩阵正交化优化器能够在实际大规模深度学习训练中高效应用，为未来模型架构的扩展提供了可行的优化方案。

Abstract: Matrix-orthogonalization-based optimizers, exemplified by Muon, have demonstrated strong convergence behavior across a wide range of modern deep learning workloads. The matrix-aware updates offer a compelling alternative to conventional element-wise optimization, particularly as model architectures continue to grow in scale and heterogeneity. Yet contemporary distributed training infrastructure built around the assumption of element-wise optimizers is poorly matched to matrix-level optimizers such as Muon, whose updates couple entire weight matrices and require costly Newton-Schulz iterations. Vanilla Muon implementations incur more than 2x the cost of forward and backward passes. To close this gap, we present DMuon, an open-source distributed Muon implementation that integrates into existing training pipelines as a drop-in module, with no framework-level modifications. Across both embodied foundation model and large language model (LLM) training workloads, DMuon achieves a 1.48x-3.01x speedup in end-to-end step time and a 6.85x-163.00x speedup in optimizer-step time, bringing per-step latency to near-AdamW levels and enabling efficient scaling in our model training.

</details>


### [29] [CHAMB-GA: A Containerized HPC Scalable Microservice-Based Framework for Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27217)
*Felix Bonhoff,Thiemo Pesch,Andrea Benigni,Alexander Mitsos,Manuel Dahmen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHAMB-GA框架，一个基于容器化的HPC可扩展微服务框架，用于遗传算法与嵌入式仿真的全局优化，实现了从个人计算机到分布式集群的无缝扩展，在超过3500个CPU核心上展示了良好的可扩展性和低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于元启发式的全局优化与嵌入式长时间仿真是一个计算昂贵的过程。现有工具链通常具有刚性和硬件绑定约束，阻碍了可扩展性和复杂仿真的集成。需要支持从个人计算机到分布式集群的无缝过渡，以支持开发和执行的各个阶段，实现跨所有计算规模的执行。

Method: 提出了CHAMB-GA（容器化HPC可扩展微服务遗传算法框架）。该框架采用容器编排技术在云基础设施上扩展，通过批处理调度的并行执行在HPC集群上扩展。用户分别提供GA算子和仿真后端，框架以分布式、解耦的方式运行这些组件，映射到分离的硬件。采用中央消息代理协调微服务之间的异步管理器-工作者通信，从而并行化进化操作和适应度评估。

Result: 通过两部分验证了CHAMB-GA的能力：(i) 基准测试显示在扩展到超过3500个CPU核心时开销最小；(ii) 德国输电网高压直流(HVDC)线路调度优化，展示了从Kubernetes到SLURM的无缝迁移、水平和垂直组合扩展、以及多阶段工作流的集成。框架证明了其可扩展性、可移植性和可重现性，同时促进了外部工具和复杂仿真的集成。

Conclusion: CHAMB-GA框架成功解决了现有工具链的刚性和硬件绑定约束问题，实现了遗传算法优化在云和HPC环境中的一致性扩展。该框架的解耦架构和消息代理机制有效地并行化了进化操作和适应度评估，为复杂仿真问题的优化提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Metaheuristic-based global optimization with embedded, long-running simulations is a computationally expensive process. To support various stages of development and execution, a seamless transition from personal computers to distributed clusters is desired, enabling execution across all computational scales. However, existing tool chains are often characterized by rigidity and hardware-bound constraints, which impede scalability and the integration of complex simulations. Bridging this gap, we present a containerized HPC scalable microservice-based framework for genetic algorithms with embedded simulations (CHAMB-GA). The deployment of the framework scales consistently across cloud infrastructure via container orchestration and HPC clusters via batch-scheduled parallel execution. Users provide the GA operators and simulation backend separately. The framework is designed to run these components in a distributed and decoupled manner, mapped to separate hardware. This approach ensures that the fitness evaluation and genetic operations are not managed within the same process and are utilizing distinct parts of the compute infrastructure. A central message broker coordinates asynchronous manager-worker communication between microservices, thereby parallelizing evolutionary operations and fitness evaluations. We demonstrate CHAMB-GA's scalability, portability, and reproducibility, while facilitating the integration of external tools and complex simulations on benchmark and powerflow problems. The capabilities of CHAMB-GA are validated in a two-part approach: (i) a benchmark study demonstrating minimal overhead while scaling to over 3,500 CPU cores, and (ii) a dispatch optimization of High Voltage Direct Current (HVDC) lines in the German transmission grid, showing seamless migration from Kubernetes to SLURM, combined horizontal and vertical scaling, and integration of multi-stage workflows.

</details>
